As a\n192: \t\t// result, we should exit the first request immediately; clients will retry.\n193: \t\tfirstRequest.Store(false)\n194: \t\treturn status.Error(codes.Unavailable, \"server warmup not complete; try again\")\n195: \t}\n196: \t// Check if server is ready to accept clients and process new requests.\n197: \t// Currently ready means caches have been synced and hence can build\n198: \t// clusters correctly. Without this check, InitContext() call below would\n199: \t// initialize with empty config, leading to reconnected Envoys losing\n200: \t// configuration. This is an additional safety check inaddition to adding\n201: ","url":"https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/sg-benchmarks/istio--4c1f845d@2ad2f397a704d647905bab3d0a26ec5fc49b7400/-/blob/pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go%3FL1-200"}}}

[]*IstioEndpoint)\\n312: \\n313: // XDSUpdater is used for direct updates of the xDS model and incremental push.\\n314: // Pilot uses multiple registries - for example each K8S cluster is a registry\\n315: // instance. Each registry is responsible for tracking a set\\n316: // of endpoints associated with mesh services, and calling the EDSUpdate on changes.\\n317: // A registry may group endpoints for a service in smaller subsets - for example by\\n318: // deployment, or to deal with very large number of endpoints for a service. *Service, Event)\n24: \n25: // Controller defines an event controller loop.  Proxy agent registers itself\n26: // with the controller loop and receives notifications on changes to the\n27: // service topology or changes to the configuration artifacts.\n28: //\n29: // The controller guarantees the following consistency requirement: registry\n30: // view in the controller is as AT LEAST as fresh as the moment notification\n31: // arrives, but MAY BE more fresh (e.g. \"delete\" cancels an \"add\" event).  For\n32: // example, an event for a service creation will see a service registry without\n33: // the service if the event is immediately followed by the service deletion\n34: // event.\n35: //\n36: // Handlers execute on the single worker queue in the order they are appended.\n37: // Handlers receive the notification event and the associated object.  Note\n38: // that all handlers must be appended before starting the controller.\n39: type Controller interface {\n40: \t// Note: AppendXXXHandler is used to register high level handlers.\n41: \t// For per cluster handlers, they should be registered by the `AppendXXXHandlerForCluster` interface.\n42: \n43: \t// AppendServiceHandler notifies about changes to the service catalog.\n44: \tAppendServiceHandler(f ServiceHandler)\n45: \n46: \t// AppendWorkloadHandler notifies about changes to workloads. This differs from InstanceHandler,\n47: \t// which deals with service instances (the result of a merge of Service and Workload)\n48: \tAppendWorkloadHandler(f func(*WorkloadInstance, Event))\n49: \n50: \t// Run until a signal is received\n51: \tRun(stop <-chan struct{})\n52: \n53: \t// HasSynced returns true after initial cache synchronization is complete\n54: \tHasSynced() bool\n55: }\n56: \n57: // AggregateController is a wrapper of Controller, it supports registering handlers of a specific cluster。\n58: type AggregateController interface {\n59: \tController\n60: \t// {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_015YXti8GbfiZsFhCQB5fN11","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CasT9vdRwyNUAmnXcuPv25","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/documentation.md","content":"# Istio Pilot Discovery Service Architecture\n\n## Overview\n\nThe Pilot Discovery Service is the heart of Istio's configuration distribution system. It serves xDS (Envoy Discovery Service) resources to all proxies in the mesh, translating Istio's high-level networking configuration (VirtualServices, DestinationRules, etc.) into Envoy-native resources (Clusters, Routes, Listeners, Endpoints) that proxies understand natively.\n\nThis document explains the architecture of Pilot's discovery subsystem—how it discovers services, translates configuration, manages proxy connections, and coordinates pushes across the fleet.\n\n---\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n### 1. DiscoveryServer: The xDS gRPC Server\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/xds/discovery.go`\n\nThe `DiscoveryServer` struct is the central gRPC server implementing Envoy's xDS v3 APIs. It is responsible for:\n\n- **Registering the gRPC service:** Implements `AggregatedDiscoveryService` interface\n- **Accepting proxy connections:** Handles new xDS streams from Envoy proxies\n- **Managing active connections:** Maintains a map of connected proxies via `adsClients`\n- **Coordinating configuration pushes:** Orchestrates when and how config updates are sent to proxies\n- **Debouncing and rate limiting:** Prevents thundering herd of configuration updates\n\n#### Key Fields\n\n```go\ntype DiscoveryServer struct {\n    Env *model.Environment                    // Access to mesh state, services, config\n    Generators map[string]model.XdsResourceGenerator  // Pluggable generators per resource type\n    ProxyNeedsPush func(proxy, req) (*PushRequest, bool)  // Filter unnecessary pushes\n\n    // Connection management\n    adsClients map[string]*Connection        // Active proxy connections\n    adsClientsMutex sync.RWMutex             // Protects adsClients\n\n    // Push coordination\n    pushChannel chan *model.PushRequest      // Debouncer input: raw config updates\n    pushQueue *PushQueue                     // Queue of proxies waiting for pushes\n    concurrentPushLimit chan struct{}        // Semaphore limiting concurrent pushes\n    RequestRateLimit *rate.Limiter           // Rate limit new connections\n\n    // Metrics\n    InboundUpdates *atomic.Int64             // Total config updates received\n    CommittedUpdates *atomic.Int64           // Updates applied to push context\n    pushVersion atomic.Uint64                // Version counter\n\n    // Cache\n    Cache model.XdsCache                     // Caches generated xDS resources\n    JwtKeyResolver *model.JwksResolver       // Handles JWT public key rotation\n}\n```\n\n#### Lifecycle\n\n1. **Creation:** `NewDiscoveryServer()` creates the server and initializes the debounce channel, push queue, and connection map\n2. **Registration:** `Register()` registers the gRPC service with the gRPC server\n3. **Startup:** `Start()` launches worker goroutines:\n   - `handleUpdates()` - debouncing thread\n   - `sendPushes()` - push worker thread\n   - `periodicRefreshMetrics()` - metrics update thread\n4. **Shutdown:** `Shutdown()` gracefully stops workers and cleans up resources\n\n#### Configuration Push Flow\n\n```\nConfigUpdate()  (from config controller)\n    ↓\npushChannel (debouncer input)\n    ↓\nhandleUpdates() (debounce logic)\n    ↓\nPush() (called after debouncing)\n    ↓\ninitPushContext() (rebuild config snapshot if full push)\n    ↓\nAdsPushAll() (send to all affected proxies)\n    ↓\npushQueue (ordered push queue)\n    ↓\nsendPushes() (concurrent push worker)\n    ↓\npushConnection() (send to single proxy)\n```\n\n#### Debouncing Strategy\n\nThe debouncing mechanism (`debounce()` function, lines 331-413) prevents excessive pushes when multiple configuration changes occur rapidly:\n\n- **DebounceAfter:** Minimum time to wait after last event before pushing (default: 100ms)\n- **debounceMax:** Maximum time to wait before pushing regardless of more events (default: 10s)\n- **enableEDSDebounce:** If false, EDS (endpoint) updates bypass debouncing for faster propagation\n\nWhen events arrive faster than the debounce interval, they merge together, reducing the total number of pushes.\n\n#### Concurrent Push Limiting\n\nTo prevent overwhelming the control plane, pushes are rate-limited:\n\n```go\nconcurrentPushLimit: make(chan struct{}, features.PushThrottle)  // Default: 100\n```\n\nThis semaphore ensures no more than N pushes run concurrently. Additional push workers block until one completes, maintaining stable resource usage.\n\n---\n\n## 2. ADS and the Connection Model\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go`\n\nADS (Aggregated Discovery Service) implements the bidirectional gRPC stream that connects a single Envoy proxy to Pilot. Each proxy connection is wrapped in a `Connection` object that manages state for that stream.\n\n### Stream Establishment\n\n```go\nfunc (s *DiscoveryServer) Stream(stream DiscoveryStream) error\n```\n\nWhen a proxy connects:\n\n1. **Authentication:** Validate mTLS identity via `authenticate()`\n2. **Server Readiness Check:** Ensure caches are synced (`IsServerReady()`)\n3. **Rate Limiting:** Respect per-proxy request limits (`WaitForRequestLimit()`)\n4. **Initial Context:** Initialize push context with current config (`InitContext()`)\n5. **Connection Wrapping:** Create a `Connection` object to wrap the stream\n6. **Stream Handler:** Delegate to `xds.Stream()` for message handling\n\n### Connection Object\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go`, lines 70-87\n\n```go\ntype Connection struct {\n    xds.Connection  // Base connection from common xDS library\n\n    node *core.Node  // Original Envoy metadata (for internal events)\n    proxy *model.Proxy  // Computed proxy metadata (identity, labels, config namespace)\n    deltaStream DeltaDiscoveryStream  // Only one of deltaStream or stream set\n    deltaReqChan chan *discovery.DeltaDiscoveryRequest\n\n    s *DiscoveryServer  // Reference to discovery server\n    ids []string  // Authentication identities (mTLS SANs)\n}\n```\n\nThe connection acts as a state machine with several key operations:\n\n- **Initialize:** `initConnection()` → `initProxyMetadata()` → `initializeProxy()`\n  - Parses Envoy metadata (cluster ID, namespace, workload labels)\n  - Authorizes the proxy\n  - Computes service targets and sidecar scopes\n  - Registers connection in `adsClients` map\n\n- **Process Requests:** `processRequest()` determines what resources the proxy needs\n  - Checks if the proxy actually needs an update (`ProxyNeedsPush`)\n  - Resolves resource names (e.g., \"service.namespace.svc.cluster.local\" → ServiceInstance list)\n  - Calls generator to produce config\n  - Sends response via `pushXds()`\n\n- **Push Updates:** `pushConnection()` sends an unsolicited update\n  - Called when infrastructure state changes (new endpoints, config updates)\n  - Generator produces config based on the proxy and push request\n  - Sends response with new version number and nonce\n\n### Request Processing\n\nWhen a proxy sends a `DiscoveryRequest`:\n\n1. **Nonce Validation:** Check response nonce matches request's last nonce (prevents stale responses)\n2. **Version Check:** If proxy's version equals server's version, no update needed\n3. **Resource Filtering:** Extract requested resource names\n4. **Generator Lookup:** Find appropriate generator for resource type (CDS, LDS, RDS, EDS, etc.)\n5. **Config Generation:** Call generator with proxy state and push context\n6. **Caching:** Cache result in XdsCache (keyed by proxy + resource type + version)\n7. **Response:** Send back `DiscoveryResponse` with resources and new version/nonce\n\n### Push Coordination\n\nWhen Pilot needs to push updates to all proxies:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *DiscoveryServer) AdsPushAll(req *model.PushRequest)\n```\n\nThis iterates all connected proxies and enqueues them for pushing:\n\n```go\nfor _, con := range s.adsClients {\n    s.pushQueue.Enqueue(con, req)\n}\n```\n\nThe queue merges multiple push requests for the same proxy, avoiding duplicate work.\n\n---\n\n## 3. PushContext: Configuration Snapshot and Translation\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/model/push_context.go`\n\n`PushContext` is an immutable snapshot of the mesh configuration created at the start of each full push. It contains all the data needed to generate config for any proxy and tracks metrics/errors during the push.\n\n### Contents\n\nA PushContext holds:\n\n```go\ntype PushContext struct {\n    // Service index by visibility and namespace\n    ServiceIndex serviceIndex\n        ├─ public []*Service  // Exported to all namespaces\n        ├─ privateByNamespace map[string][]*Service  // Private to namespace\n        └─ exportedToNamespace map[string][]*Service  // Exported to specific namespace\n\n    // Istio config indexes\n    virtualServiceIndex virtualServiceIndex  // VirtualServices by gateway/namespace\n    destinationRuleIndex destinationRuleIndex  // DestinationRules for each service\n    gatewayIndex gatewayIndex  // Gateways by namespace\n    sidecarIndex sidecarIndex  // SidecarScopes by namespace\n\n    // Policies and filters\n    AuthnPolicies *AuthenticationPolicies  // RequestAuthentication, PeerAuthentication\n    AuthzPolicies *AuthorizationPolicies  // AuthorizationPolicy rules\n    Telemetry *Telemetries  // Telemetry resources (observability config)\n    ProxyConfigs *ProxyConfigs  // ProxyConfig resources\n    envoyFiltersByNamespace map[string][]*EnvoyFilterWrapper  // EnvoyFilter patches\n    wasmPluginsByNamespace map[string][]*WasmPluginWrapper  // WasmPlugin extensions\n\n    // Metadata\n    Mesh *meshconfig.MeshConfig  // Global mesh settings\n    Networks *meshconfig.MeshNetworks  // Multi-cluster network info\n    PushVersion string  // Unique version identifier (timestamp + counter)\n\n    // Error tracking\n    ProxyStatus map[string]map[string]ProxyPushStatus  // Errors per proxy/metric\n    InitDone atomic.Bool  // Initialization completion marker\n}\n```\n\n### Initialization Pipeline\n\nWhen a full push occurs, `InitContext()` rebuilds the PushContext (lines 1338-1366):\n\n```go\nfunc (ps *PushContext) InitContext(env, oldPushContext, pushReq)\n```\n\n1. **Lock:** Acquire mutex to prevent concurrent initialization\n2. **Load Mesh Config:** `ps.Mesh = env.Mesh()`\n3. **Initialize Export Defaults:** Set default visibility (public/private)\n4. **Decide Update Strategy:**\n   - **Full rebuild** if: no old context, forced push, or new Istio config\n   - **Incremental update** if: old context exists and config change is targeted\n\n#### Full Rebuild (`createNewContext()`)\n\nFor fresh starts or major config changes:\n\n1. **initServiceRegistry()** - Load all Services and their endpoints from registries\n2. **initKubernetesGateways()** - Initialize Gateway API resources\n3. **initVirtualServices()** - Index all VirtualServices by hostname/gateway\n4. **initDestinationRules()** - Index DestinationRules, merge by hostname\n5. **initAuthnPolicies()** - Load RequestAuthentication and PeerAuthentication\n6. **initAuthorizationPolicies()** - Index AuthorizationPolicy by namespace/labels\n7. **initTelemetry()** - Process Telemetry resources for observability config\n8. **initProxyConfigs()** - Load ProxyConfig resources\n9. **initWasmPlugins()** - Index WASM plugins by namespace\n10. **initEnvoyFilters()** - Index EnvoyFilters for proxy patching\n11. **initGateways()** - Index Istio Gateway resources\n12. **initAmbient()** - Initialize ambient mesh data structures\n13. **initSidecarScopes()** - Compute per-proxy visibility (must be last)\n\n#### Incremental Update (`updateContext()`)\n\nReuses old indexes for unchanged config types:\n\n- Detects what changed: `for conf := range pushReq.ConfigsUpdated`\n- Only rebuilds affected indexes (e.g., if VirtualService changed, rebuild virtualServiceIndex)\n- Copies unchanged indexes from old context\n- Helps with performance when changes are localized\n\n### Config Translation: From Istio to Envoy\n\nGenerators translate Istio config to Envoy via `ConfigGenerator` interface. Key generators:\n\n#### CDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/cds.go`)\n\nTranslates Services, VirtualServices, and DestinationRules → Envoy Clusters:\n\n```go\ntype CdsGenerator struct {\n    ConfigGenerator core.ConfigGenerator\n}\n\nfunc (c CdsGenerator) Generate(proxy, watched, req) (Resources, error) {\n    return c.ConfigGenerator.BuildClusters(proxy, req, watched.ResourceNames)\n}\n```\n\n**Filter:** Skips configs that don't impact clusters (e.g., Gateway, AuthorizationPolicy)\n\n#### RDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/rds.go`)\n\nTranslates VirtualServices and DestinationRules → Envoy Routes:\n\n```go\nfunc (c RdsGenerator) Generate(proxy, watched, req) (Resources, error) {\n    return c.ConfigGenerator.BuildHTTPRoutes(proxy, req, watched.ResourceNames)\n}\n```\n\n**Filter:** Skips configs that don't impact routes (e.g., WorkloadEntry, RequestAuthentication)\n\n#### LDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/lds.go`)\n\nTranslates Sidecars, Gateways, and VirtualServices → Envoy Listeners\n\n#### EDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/eds.go`)\n\nTranslates ServiceInstances and Endpoints → Envoy Endpoints (load balancing destinations)\n\n### Translation Process\n\nFor each proxy, generators follow this pattern:\n\n1. **Read Config:** Access PushContext indexes (VirtualServices, DestinationRules, etc.)\n2. **Filter by Scope:** Apply sidecar scope, namespace, RBAC rules\n3. **Merge Config:** Combine multiple resources (e.g., merged DestinationRules)\n4. **Transform:** Convert Istio semantics to Envoy equivalents\n   - VirtualService routes → Envoy RouteConfiguration\n   - DestinationRule subsets → Envoy Cluster subsets with labels\n   - ServiceEntry → Envoy Cluster with static/DNS endpoints\n5. **Apply Patches:** Merge EnvoyFilter patches\n6. **Cache:** Store computed result keyed by (proxy, resource-type, push-version)\n\n### Sidecar Scopes\n\n**Critical for scalability:** Sidecar resources define which services each proxy can reach, enabling:\n\n- Per-proxy visibility filtering (only send needed clusters/routes)\n- Namespace-level isolation and multitenancy\n- Waypoint/gateway specific configurations\n\nComputed in `initSidecarScopes()` and cached on the proxy:\n\n```go\nproxy.SidecarScope = ps.GetSidecarScope(proxy, ns)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Service Registry and Multi-Cluster Support\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/model/service.go`, `pilot/pkg/model/controller.go`\n\nThe service registry layer abstracts away platform specifics (Kubernetes, VMs, external services) and provides a unified view of services in the mesh.\n\n### Service Model\n\n```go\ntype Service struct {\n    Hostname host.Name  // FQDN (e.g., \"svc.default.svc.cluster.local\")\n    Ports PortList  // Listening ports\n\n    Attributes ServiceAttributes\n        ├─ Name string  // Short name\n        ├─ Namespace string  // Kubernetes namespace\n        ├─ Labels map[string]string  // User labels\n        ├─ ExportTo visibility.Instance  // Who can see this service\n        └─ TrafficDistribution string  // Locality-aware LB policy\n\n    ClusterVIPs AddressMap  // VIP per cluster (multi-cluster support)\n    Resolution Resolution  // ClientSideLB, DNSLB, Passthrough, etc.\n    MeshExternal bool  // True if external to mesh (ServiceEntry)\n}\n```\n\nEach service can have multiple endpoints (IstioEndpoints) across clusters:\n\n```go\ntype IstioEndpoint struct {\n    Address string  // Pod IP or external IP\n    Port int  // Service port\n    Network string  // Cluster network\n    Labels labels.Instance  // Pod labels (subset selection)\n    Locality *core.Locality  // Zone/region\n}\n```\n\n### Controller Interface\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/model/controller.go`\n\n```go\ntype Controller interface {\n    AppendServiceHandler(f ServiceHandler)  // Subscribe to service changes\n    AppendWorkloadHandler(f func(*WorkloadInstance, Event))  // Subscribe to workload changes\n    Run(stop <-chan struct{})  // Start the controller\n    HasSynced() bool  // Ready to serve requests\n}\n\ntype ServiceHandler func(prev, curr *Service, event Event)\n```\n\nControllers watch Kubernetes/VM resources and notify Pilot when:\n- **EventAdd:** New service created\n- **EventUpdate:** Service modified (ports, labels, endpoints)\n- **EventDelete:** Service removed\n\n### Aggregated Registry Pattern\n\nPilot supports multiple registries (per cluster in multi-cluster, or multiple connectors):\n\n```go\ntype AggregateController interface {\n    Controller\n    AppendServiceHandlerForCluster(clusterID, handler)\n    UnRegisterHandlersForCluster(clusterID)\n}\n```\n\nWhen a service update arrives from any cluster:\n\n1. **Handler fires** (e.g., Kubernetes controller detects endpoint change)\n2. **EDSUpdate or SvcUpdate** called on XDSUpdater (DiscoveryServer implements this)\n3. **PushRequest generated** with trigger reason (EndpointUpdate, ServiceUpdate, etc.)\n4. **Debouncer receives** the request\n5. **Affected proxies** enqueued for push\n\n### Multi-Cluster Data Flow\n\nIn a multi-cluster mesh:\n\n1. **Per-cluster Pilot instances** run in each cluster\n2. **Remote secret watchers** connect to remote API servers\n3. **Services aggregated** from all clusters into unified ServiceIndex\n4. **ClusterVIPs** stored per cluster (same FQDN, different VIPs in each cluster)\n5. **Endpoints aggregated** across clusters, sorted by network locality\n6. **Proxies in one cluster** receive routes to services in all clusters\n\nExample endpoint distribution:\n\n```\nService: productpage.default.svc.cluster.local\n├─ Cluster A (network: network-a)\n│  └─ Pod 10.1.1.1:9080 (zone: us-west-1a)\n│  └─ Pod 10.1.2.1:9080 (zone: us-west-1b)\n├─ Cluster B (network: network-b)\n│  └─ Pod 10.2.1.1:9080 (zone: us-east-1a)\n```\n\nWhen a proxy in Cluster A requests endpoints:\n\n1. Local endpoints prioritized (same network)\n2. Remote endpoints included with gateway (cross-cluster LB traffic)\n3. Network labels used for traffic policies\n\n### Service Discovery Sources\n\n#### Kubernetes Registries\n\n- **K8s Services** → Service.Hostname, VIP\n- **K8s Endpoints** → IstioEndpoint list with pod labels/IPs\n- **K8s Pods** → Locality (zone labels), workload identity\n- **Labels** → Subset selection in DestinationRule\n\n#### ServiceEntry Resources\n\nCustom services (external, VMs):\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1alpha3\nkind: ServiceEntry\nmetadata:\n  name: external-api\nspec:\n  hosts:\n  - api.external.com\n  ports:\n  - number: 443\n    name: https\n  location: MESH_EXTERNAL\n  endpoints:\n  - address: 192.0.2.1  # External IP\n    ports:\n      https: 443\n```\n\nTreated like any other service, but endpoints are static/DNS.\n\n#### Workload Entry Resources\n\nVMs or external hosts:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1alpha3\nkind: WorkloadEntry\nmetadata:\n  name: vm-1\n  namespace: default\nspec:\n  address: 192.0.2.100\n  labels:\n    app: database\n    version: v1\n```\n\nMerged into service endpoints, participate in Sidecar and DestinationRule scoping.\n\n### Service Registry Triggers\n\nWhen endpoints change, `EDSUpdate` enqueues only affected proxies:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *DiscoveryServer) EDSUpdate(shard, hostname, namespace, endpoints)\n```\n\nThis avoids full pushes—only EDS (endpoint list) is recomputed:\n\n- **Trigger:** Pod scaled up/down, health changed\n- **Reason:** EndpointUpdate\n- **Full:** false (incremental update)\n- **ConfigsUpdated:** empty (means all proxies potentially affected, but only EDS recomputed)\n\nThis keeps endpoint changes fast and lightweight.\n\n---\n\n## Error Handling\n\n### Connection Errors\n\n**Scenarios:**\n- Proxy disconnects unexpectedly → `closeConnection()` removes from `adsClients`\n- Invalid metadata → return error, reject connection\n- Authorization fails → return error, deny access\n\n**Recovery:** Proxy reconnects with backoff; no state loss (stateless design)\n\n### Push Errors\n\n**Scenarios:**\n- Generator panics → recover and log error\n- Cache miss (shouldn't happen) → regenerate on the fly\n- Network error sending response → connection drops, proxy reconnects\n\n**Tracking:** Errors recorded in `PushContext.ProxyStatus[metricName]` for debugging\n\n### Config Errors\n\n**Scenarios:**\n- Invalid VirtualService → Validation errors logged but push continues\n- Circular service references → Detection and logging\n- Missing DestinationRule → Graceful fallback to default behavior\n\n**Mechanism:** Dedicated validation phase before push; errors recorded and exposed on debug endpoint\n\n---\n\n## Extension Points\n\n### Custom Generators\n\nOverride default resource generation:\n\n```go\nserver.Generators[\"my-custom\"] = &MyCustomGenerator{...}\n```\n\nProxy metadata `Generator: \"my-custom\"` triggers custom generator.\n\n### Custom Controllers\n\nAdd new service discovery sources:\n\n```go\nenv.ServiceDiscovery.(*aggregate.Controller).AddRegistry(myRegistry)\n```\n\nImplement `Controller` interface, register handlers.\n\n### ProxyNeedsPush Filter\n\nSkip pushes for proxies that don't need updates:\n\n```go\nserver.ProxyNeedsPush = func(proxy *model.Proxy, req *model.PushRequest) (*model.PushRequest, bool) {\n    // Custom logic: can we skip this push?\n    if req.Full && !proxy.NeedsFull() {\n        return req, false  // Skip\n    }\n    return req, true  // Send\n}\n```\n\n### EnvoyFilter Integration\n\nPatch generated Envoy config per proxy:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1alpha3\nkind: EnvoyFilter\nmetadata:\n  name: auth-patch\nspec:\n  workloadSelector:\n    labels:\n      app: frontend\n  configPatches:\n  - applyTo: LISTENER\n    patch:\n      operation: MERGE\n      value:\n        # Merge custom listener config\n```\n\n---\n\n## Data Flow Example: Service Creation to Proxy Update\n\n### Scenario\nA new Kubernetes Service `shipping.default` is created with 2 pods.\n\n### Flow\n\n```\n1. Kubernetes API Server\n   └─ Event: Service \"shipping\" created\n\n2. K8s Service Registry Watcher\n   └─ Handler: onServiceCreate()\n\n3. XDSUpdater.SvcUpdate()\n   ├─ Reason: ServiceUpdate\n   └─ → DiscoveryServer.ConfigUpdate()\n\n4. DiscoveryServer.ConfigUpdate()\n   ├─ Increment InboundUpdates counter\n   ├─ Send to pushChannel\n   └─ (May be debounced)\n\n5. handleUpdates() (debounce goroutine)\n   ├─ Wait: DebounceAfter time since last event\n   ├─ Call: Push(req)\n   └─ Increment CommittedUpdates counter\n\n6. DiscoveryServer.Push()\n   ├─ Is Full push? YES (service is global config)\n   ├─ Call: initPushContext()\n   │  ├─ NewPushContext created\n   │  ├─ InitContext() called:\n   │  │  ├─ createNewContext() (or updateContext if incremental)\n   │  │  ├─ initServiceRegistry() loads Services (including new \"shipping\")\n   │  │  ├─ initDestinationRules()\n   │  │  ├─ initVirtualServices()\n   │  │  └─ initSidecarScopes() computes per-proxy visibility\n   │  └─ Store in Env.pushContext\n   ├─ dropCacheForRequest() clear old xDS cache\n   └─ Call: AdsPushAll(req)\n\n7. AdsPushAll()\n   ├─ For each proxy in adsClients:\n   │  └─ pushQueue.Enqueue(proxy, req)\n   └─ Proxies queued\n\n8. sendPushes() (push worker goroutine)\n   ├─ Acquire concurrentPushLimit semaphore\n   ├─ Dequeue proxy from pushQueue\n   ├─ Call: pushConnection(proxy, pushReq)\n   │  ├─ For each watched resource type (CDS, RDS, EDS):\n   │  │  ├─ Call: CdsGenerator.Generate(proxy, req)\n   │  │  │  ├─ Access: ps.ServiceIndex (includes new \"shipping\")\n   │  │  │  ├─ Filter by proxy SidecarScope (can proxy reach \"shipping\"?)\n   │  │  │  ├─ Transform: Service → Envoy Cluster\n   │  │  │  ├─ Cache result\n   │  │  │  └─ Return Cluster resources\n   │  │  └─ Build response\n   │  └─ Send DiscoveryResponse via gRPC\n   ├─ Queue marks done\n   └─ Release semaphore\n\n9. Envoy Proxy Receives\n   ├─ Response: clusters=[..., {name: \"outbound|80||shipping.default.svc.cluster.local\", ...}]\n   ├─ Version: \"2024-02-21T14:30:45Z/12345\"\n   ├─ Nonce: \"uuid-xyz\"\n   └─ Proxy ACKs with same nonce\n\n10. Proxy Configuration Updated\n    ├─ New cluster routing to \"shipping.default\" endpoints\n    ├─ Future requests sent to shipping pods\n    └─ No manual intervention needed\n```\n\n---\n\n## Key Performance Characteristics\n\n### Scalability Features\n\n1. **Incremental Pushes:** Only affected proxies enqueued (via SidecarScope filtering)\n2. **Debouncing:** Multiple config changes merged into single push\n3. **Concurrent Push Limiting:** Prevents thundering herd, steady resource usage\n4. **Request Rate Limiting:** Per-connection rate limiter on new xDS requests\n5. **Caching:** xDS resources cached by (proxy, resource-type, version)\n6. **Index Structures:** ServiceIndex, virtualServiceIndex, etc. enable O(1) lookups\n\n### Timing\n\n- **Debounce latency:** 100ms–10s (configurable)\n- **Push time:** Typically < 1s for 100-1000 proxies\n- **Connection init:** ~100ms (includes service target computation)\n\n---\n\n## Relevant Source Files\n\n### Core Components\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/discovery.go` — DiscoveryServer, push coordination, debouncing\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go` — ADS stream handler, Connection, request processing\n- `pilot/pkg/model/push_context.go` — PushContext, config initialization, indexes\n- `pilot/pkg/model/context.go` — Environment (push context holder)\n\n### Generators & Translation\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/cds.go` — Cluster Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/lds.go` — Listener Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/rds.go` — Route Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/eds.go` — Endpoint Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/networking/core/` — ConfigGenerator (main translation logic)\n\n### Service Registry & Discovery\n- `pilot/pkg/model/service.go` — Service, ServiceInstance, Resolution types\n- `pilot/pkg/model/controller.go` — Controller interface, event model\n- `pilot/pkg/serviceregistry/` — Kubernetes controller implementation\n\n### Queue & Concurrency\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/pushqueue.go` — PushQueue, proxy push ordering\n\n### Configuration Models\n- `pilot/pkg/model/destination_rule.go` — DestinationRule handling\n- `pilot/pkg/model/virtualservice.go` — VirtualService handling\n- `pilot/pkg/model/sidecar.go` — SidecarScope computation\n- `pilot/pkg/model/authentication.go` — AuthN/AuthZ policy models\n\n### Multi-Cluster\n- `pilot/pkg/model/network.go` — Network topology for multi-cluster\n- Cluster registry aggregation in ServiceDiscovery\n\n---\n\n## Conclusion\n\nPilot's Discovery Service achieves high performance and scalability through:\n\n1. **Separation of concerns:** DiscoveryServer handles gRPC; PushContext holds config snapshot; Generators handle translation\n2. **Smart filtering:** SidecarScope limits what each proxy needs; incremental pushes reduce work\n3. **Debouncing & rate limiting:** Prevents thrashing when many config changes occur\n4. **Pluggable architecture:** Custom generators, controllers, and filters enable extensions\n5. **Stateless design:** Proxies reconnect without data loss; connection state rebuilt from config\n\nThe architecture elegantly scales to thousands of proxies and supports advanced features like multi-cluster, ambient mesh, and traffic management with minimal manual tuning.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":9,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2816,"cache_read_input_tokens":88397,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2816},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4154eabc-65d9-4f92-b9fe-e4a93bc0d33a","uuid":"070a4bc7-adfc-4c7a-a8dc-f8f450ea9ff0"}

It serves xDS (Envoy Discovery Service) resources to all proxies in the mesh, translating Istio's high-level networking configuration (VirtualServices, DestinationRules, etc.) into Envoy-native resources (Clusters, Routes, Listeners, Endpoints) that proxies understand natively.\n\nThis document explains the architecture of Pilot's discovery subsystem—how it discovers services, translates configuration, manages proxy connections, and coordinates pushes across the fleet.\n\n---\n\n## Component Responsibilities\n\n### 1. DiscoveryServer: The xDS gRPC Server\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/xds/discovery.go`\n\nThe `DiscoveryServer` struct is the central gRPC server implementing Envoy's xDS v3 APIs. It is responsible for:\n\n- **Registering the gRPC service:** Implements `AggregatedDiscoveryService` interface\n- **Accepting proxy connections:** Handles new xDS streams from Envoy proxies\n- **Managing active connections:** Maintains a map of connected proxies via `adsClients`\n- **Coordinating configuration pushes:** Orchestrates when and how config updates are sent to proxies\n- **Debouncing and rate limiting:** Prevents thundering herd of configuration updates\n\n#### Key Fields\n\n```go\ntype DiscoveryServer struct {\n    Env *model.Environment                    // Access to mesh state, services, config\n    Generators map[string]model.XdsResourceGenerator  // Pluggable generators per resource type\n    ProxyNeedsPush func(proxy, req) (*PushRequest, bool)  // Filter unnecessary pushes\n\n    // Connection management\n    adsClients map[string]*Connection        // Active proxy connections\n    adsClientsMutex sync.RWMutex             // Protects adsClients\n\n    // Push coordination\n    pushChannel chan *model.PushRequest      // Debouncer input: raw config updates\n    pushQueue *PushQueue                     // Queue of proxies waiting for pushes\n    concurrentPushLimit chan struct{}        // Semaphore limiting concurrent pushes\n    RequestRateLimit *rate.Limiter           // Rate limit new connections\n\n    // Metrics\n    InboundUpdates *atomic.Int64             // Total config updates received\n    CommittedUpdates *atomic.Int64           // Updates applied to push context\n    pushVersion atomic.Uint64                // Version counter\n\n    // Cache\n    Cache model.XdsCache                     // Caches generated xDS resources\n    JwtKeyResolver *model.JwksResolver       // Handles JWT public key rotation\n}\n```\n\n#### Lifecycle\n\n1. **Creation:** `NewDiscoveryServer()` creates the server and initializes the debounce channel, push queue, and connection map\n2. **Registration:** `Register()` registers the gRPC service with the gRPC server\n3. **Startup:** `Start()` launches worker goroutines:\n   - `handleUpdates()` - debouncing thread\n   - `sendPushes()` - push worker thread\n   - `periodicRefreshMetrics()` - metrics update thread\n4. **Shutdown:** `Shutdown()` gracefully stops workers and cleans up resources\n\n#### Configuration Push Flow\n\n```\nConfigUpdate()  (from config controller)\n    ↓\npushChannel (debouncer input)\n    ↓\nhandleUpdates() (debounce logic)\n    ↓\nPush() (called after debouncing)\n    ↓\ninitPushContext() (rebuild config snapshot if full push)\n    ↓\nAdsPushAll() (send to all affected proxies)\n    ↓\npushQueue (ordered push queue)\n    ↓\nsendPushes() (concurrent push worker)\n    ↓\npushConnection() (send to single proxy)\n```\n\n#### Debouncing Strategy\n\nThe debouncing mechanism (`debounce()` function, lines 331-413) prevents excessive pushes when multiple configuration changes occur rapidly:\n\n- **DebounceAfter:** Minimum time to wait after last event before pushing (default: 100ms)\n- **debounceMax:** Maximum time to wait before pushing regardless of more events (default: 10s)\n- **enableEDSDebounce:** If false, EDS (endpoint) updates bypass debouncing for faster propagation\n\nWhen events arrive faster than the debounce interval, they merge together, reducing the total number of pushes.\n\n#### Concurrent Push Limiting\n\nTo prevent overwhelming the control plane, pushes are rate-limited:\n\n```go\nconcurrentPushLimit: make(chan struct{}, features.PushThrottle)  // Default: 100\n```\n\nThis semaphore ensures no more than N pushes run concurrently. Additional push workers block until one completes, maintaining stable resource usage.\n\n---\n\n## 2. ADS and the Connection Model\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go`\n\nADS (Aggregated Discovery Service) implements the bidirectional gRPC stream that connects a single Envoy proxy to Pilot. Each proxy connection is wrapped in a `Connection` object that manages state for that stream.\n\n### Stream Establishment\n\n```go\nfunc (s *DiscoveryServer) Stream(stream DiscoveryStream) error\n```\n\nWhen a proxy connects:\n\n1. **Authentication:** Validate mTLS identity via `authenticate()`\n2. **Server Readiness Check:** Ensure caches are synced (`IsServerReady()`)\n3. **Rate Limiting:** Respect per-proxy request limits (`WaitForRequestLimit()`)\n4. **Initial Context:** Initialize push context with current config (`InitContext()`)\n5. **Connection Wrapping:** Create a `Connection` object to wrap the stream\n6. **Stream Handler:** Delegate to `xds.Stream()` for message handling\n\n### Connection Object\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go`, lines 70-87\n\n```go\ntype Connection struct {\n    xds.Connection  // Base connection from common xDS library\n\n    node *core.Node  // Original Envoy metadata (for internal events)\n    proxy *model.Proxy  // Computed proxy metadata (identity, labels, config namespace)\n    deltaStream DeltaDiscoveryStream  // Only one of deltaStream or stream set\n    deltaReqChan chan *discovery.DeltaDiscoveryRequest\n\n    s *DiscoveryServer  // Reference to discovery server\n    ids []string  // Authentication identities (mTLS SANs)\n}\n```\n\nThe connection acts as a state machine with several key operations:\n\n- **Initialize:** `initConnection()` → `initProxyMetadata()` → `initializeProxy()`\n  - Parses Envoy metadata (cluster ID, namespace, workload labels)\n  - Authorizes the proxy\n  - Computes service targets and sidecar scopes\n  - Registers connection in `adsClients` map\n\n- **Process Requests:** `processRequest()` determines what resources the proxy needs\n  - Checks if the proxy actually needs an update (`ProxyNeedsPush`)\n  - Resolves resource names (e.g., \"service.namespace.svc.cluster.local\" → ServiceInstance list)\n  - Calls generator to produce config\n  - Sends response via `pushXds()`\n\n- **Push Updates:** `pushConnection()` sends an unsolicited update\n  - Called when infrastructure state changes (new endpoints, config updates)\n  - Generator produces config based on the proxy and push request\n  - Sends response with new version number and nonce\n\n### Request Processing\n\nWhen a proxy sends a `DiscoveryRequest`:\n\n1. **Nonce Validation:** Check response nonce matches request's last nonce (prevents stale responses)\n2. **Version Check:** If proxy's version equals server's version, no update needed\n3. **Resource Filtering:** Extract requested resource names\n4. **Generator Lookup:** Find appropriate generator for resource type (CDS, LDS, RDS, EDS, etc.)\n5. **Config Generation:** Call generator with proxy state and push context\n6. **Caching:** Cache result in XdsCache (keyed by proxy + resource type + version)\n7. **Response:** Send back `DiscoveryResponse` with resources and new version/nonce\n\n### Push Coordination\n\nWhen Pilot needs to push updates to all proxies:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *DiscoveryServer) AdsPushAll(req *model.PushRequest)\n```\n\nThis iterates all connected proxies and enqueues them for pushing:\n\n```go\nfor _, con := range s.adsClients {\n    s.pushQueue.Enqueue(con, req)\n}\n```\n\nThe queue merges multiple push requests for the same proxy, avoiding duplicate work.\n\n---\n\n## 3. PushContext: Configuration Snapshot and Translation\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/model/push_context.go`\n\n`PushContext` is an immutable snapshot of the mesh configuration created at the start of each full push. It contains all the data needed to generate config for any proxy and tracks metrics/errors during the push.\n\n### Contents\n\nA PushContext holds:\n\n```go\ntype PushContext struct {\n    // Service index by visibility and namespace\n    ServiceIndex serviceIndex\n        ├─ public []*Service  // Exported to all namespaces\n        ├─ privateByNamespace map[string][]*Service  // Private to namespace\n        └─ exportedToNamespace map[string][]*Service  // Exported to specific namespace\n\n    // Istio config indexes\n    virtualServiceIndex virtualServiceIndex  // VirtualServices by gateway/namespace\n    destinationRuleIndex destinationRuleIndex  // DestinationRules for each service\n    gatewayIndex gatewayIndex  // Gateways by namespace\n    sidecarIndex sidecarIndex  // SidecarScopes by namespace\n\n    // Policies and filters\n    AuthnPolicies *AuthenticationPolicies  // RequestAuthentication, PeerAuthentication\n    AuthzPolicies *AuthorizationPolicies  // AuthorizationPolicy rules\n    Telemetry *Telemetries  // Telemetry resources (observability config)\n    ProxyConfigs *ProxyConfigs  // ProxyConfig resources\n    envoyFiltersByNamespace map[string][]*EnvoyFilterWrapper  // EnvoyFilter patches\n    wasmPluginsByNamespace map[string][]*WasmPluginWrapper  // WasmPlugin extensions\n\n    // Metadata\n    Mesh *meshconfig.MeshConfig  // Global mesh settings\n    Networks *meshconfig.MeshNetworks  // Multi-cluster network info\n    PushVersion string  // Unique version identifier (timestamp + counter)\n\n    // Error tracking\n    ProxyStatus map[string]map[string]ProxyPushStatus  // Errors per proxy/metric\n    InitDone atomic.Bool  // Initialization completion marker\n}\n```\n\n### Initialization Pipeline\n\nWhen a full push occurs, `InitContext()` rebuilds the PushContext (lines 1338-1366):\n\n```go\nfunc (ps *PushContext) InitContext(env, oldPushContext, pushReq)\n```\n\n1. **Lock:** Acquire mutex to prevent concurrent initialization\n2. **Load Mesh Config:** `ps.Mesh = env.Mesh()`\n3. **Initialize Export Defaults:** Set default visibility (public/private)\n4. **Decide Update Strategy:**\n   - **Full rebuild** if: no old context, forced push, or new Istio config\n   - **Incremental update** if: old context exists and config change is targeted\n\n#### Full Rebuild (`createNewContext()`)\n\nFor fresh starts or major config changes:\n\n1. **initServiceRegistry()** - Load all Services and their endpoints from registries\n2. **initKubernetesGateways()** - Initialize Gateway API resources\n3. **initVirtualServices()** - Index all VirtualServices by hostname/gateway\n4. **initDestinationRules()** - Index DestinationRules, merge by hostname\n5. **initAuthnPolicies()** - Load RequestAuthentication and PeerAuthentication\n6. **initAuthorizationPolicies()** - Index AuthorizationPolicy by namespace/labels\n7. **initTelemetry()** - Process Telemetry resources for observability config\n8. **initProxyConfigs()** - Load ProxyConfig resources\n9. **initWasmPlugins()** - Index WASM plugins by namespace\n10. **initEnvoyFilters()** - Index EnvoyFilters for proxy patching\n11. **initGateways()** - Index Istio Gateway resources\n12. **initAmbient()** - Initialize ambient mesh data structures\n13. **initSidecarScopes()** - Compute per-proxy visibility (must be last)\n\n#### Incremental Update (`updateContext()`)\n\nReuses old indexes for unchanged config types:\n\n- Detects what changed: `for conf := range pushReq.ConfigsUpdated`\n- Only rebuilds affected indexes (e.g., if VirtualService changed, rebuild virtualServiceIndex)\n- Copies unchanged indexes from old context\n- Helps with performance when changes are localized\n\n### Config Translation: From Istio to Envoy\n\nGenerators translate Istio config to Envoy via `ConfigGenerator` interface. Key generators:\n\n#### CDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/cds.go`)\n\nTranslates Services, VirtualServices, and DestinationRules → Envoy Clusters:\n\n```go\ntype CdsGenerator struct {\n    ConfigGenerator core.ConfigGenerator\n}\n\nfunc (c CdsGenerator) Generate(proxy, watched, req) (Resources, error) {\n    return c.ConfigGenerator.BuildClusters(proxy, req, watched.ResourceNames)\n}\n```\n\n**Filter:** Skips configs that don't impact clusters (e.g., Gateway, AuthorizationPolicy)\n\n#### RDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/rds.go`)\n\nTranslates VirtualServices and DestinationRules → Envoy Routes:\n\n```go\nfunc (c RdsGenerator) Generate(proxy, watched, req) (Resources, error) {\n    return c.ConfigGenerator.BuildHTTPRoutes(proxy, req, watched.ResourceNames)\n}\n```\n\n**Filter:** Skips configs that don't impact routes (e.g., WorkloadEntry, RequestAuthentication)\n\n#### LDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/lds.go`)\n\nTranslates Sidecars, Gateways, and VirtualServices → Envoy Listeners\n\n#### EDS Generator (`pilot/pkg/xds/eds.go`)\n\nTranslates ServiceInstances and Endpoints → Envoy Endpoints (load balancing destinations)\n\n### Translation Process\n\nFor each proxy, generators follow this pattern:\n\n1. **Read Config:** Access PushContext indexes (VirtualServices, DestinationRules, etc.)\n2. **Filter by Scope:** Apply sidecar scope, namespace, RBAC rules\n3. **Merge Config:** Combine multiple resources (e.g., merged DestinationRules)\n4. **Transform:** Convert Istio semantics to Envoy equivalents\n   - VirtualService routes → Envoy RouteConfiguration\n   - DestinationRule subsets → Envoy Cluster subsets with labels\n   - ServiceEntry → Envoy Cluster with static/DNS endpoints\n5. **Apply Patches:** Merge EnvoyFilter patches\n6. **Cache:** Store computed result keyed by (proxy, resource-type, push-version)\n\n### Sidecar Scopes\n\n**Critical for scalability:** Sidecar resources define which services each proxy can reach, enabling:\n\n- Per-proxy visibility filtering (only send needed clusters/routes)\n- Namespace-level isolation and multitenancy\n- Waypoint/gateway specific configurations\n\nComputed in `initSidecarScopes()` and cached on the proxy:\n\n```go\nproxy.SidecarScope = ps.GetSidecarScope(proxy, ns)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Service Registry and Multi-Cluster Support\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/model/service.go`, `pilot/pkg/model/controller.go`\n\nThe service registry layer abstracts away platform specifics (Kubernetes, VMs, external services) and provides a unified view of services in the mesh.\n\n### Service Model\n\n```go\ntype Service struct {\n    Hostname host.Name  // FQDN (e.g., \"svc.default.svc.cluster.local\")\n    Ports PortList  // Listening ports\n\n    Attributes ServiceAttributes\n        ├─ Name string  // Short name\n        ├─ Namespace string  // Kubernetes namespace\n        ├─ Labels map[string]string  // User labels\n        ├─ ExportTo visibility.Instance  // Who can see this service\n        └─ TrafficDistribution string  // Locality-aware LB policy\n\n    ClusterVIPs AddressMap  // VIP per cluster (multi-cluster support)\n    Resolution Resolution  // ClientSideLB, DNSLB, Passthrough, etc.\n    MeshExternal bool  // True if external to mesh (ServiceEntry)\n}\n```\n\nEach service can have multiple endpoints (IstioEndpoints) across clusters:\n\n```go\ntype IstioEndpoint struct {\n    Address string  // Pod IP or external IP\n    Port int  // Service port\n    Network string  // Cluster network\n    Labels labels.Instance  // Pod labels (subset selection)\n    Locality *core.Locality  // Zone/region\n}\n```\n\n### Controller Interface\n\n**Location:** `pilot/pkg/model/controller.go`\n\n```go\ntype Controller interface {\n    AppendServiceHandler(f ServiceHandler)  // Subscribe to service changes\n    AppendWorkloadHandler(f func(*WorkloadInstance, Event))  // Subscribe to workload changes\n    Run(stop <-chan struct{})  // Start the controller\n    HasSynced() bool  // Ready to serve requests\n}\n\ntype ServiceHandler func(prev, curr *Service, event Event)\n```\n\nControllers watch Kubernetes/VM resources and notify Pilot when:\n- **EventAdd:** New service created\n- **EventUpdate:** Service modified (ports, labels, endpoints)\n- **EventDelete:** Service removed\n\n### Aggregated Registry Pattern\n\nPilot supports multiple registries (per cluster in multi-cluster, or multiple connectors):\n\n```go\ntype AggregateController interface {\n    Controller\n    AppendServiceHandlerForCluster(clusterID, handler)\n    UnRegisterHandlersForCluster(clusterID)\n}\n```\n\nWhen a service update arrives from any cluster:\n\n1. **Handler fires** (e.g., Kubernetes controller detects endpoint change)\n2. **EDSUpdate or SvcUpdate** called on XDSUpdater (DiscoveryServer implements this)\n3. **PushRequest generated** with trigger reason (EndpointUpdate, ServiceUpdate, etc.)\n4. **Debouncer receives** the request\n5. **Affected proxies** enqueued for push\n\n### Multi-Cluster Data Flow\n\nIn a multi-cluster mesh:\n\n1. **Per-cluster Pilot instances** run in each cluster\n2. **Remote secret watchers** connect to remote API servers\n3. **Services aggregated** from all clusters into unified ServiceIndex\n4. **ClusterVIPs** stored per cluster (same FQDN, different VIPs in each cluster)\n5. **Endpoints aggregated** across clusters, sorted by network locality\n6. **Proxies in one cluster** receive routes to services in all clusters\n\nExample endpoint distribution:\n\n```\nService: productpage.default.svc.cluster.local\n├─ Cluster A (network: network-a)\n│  └─ Pod 10.1.1.1:9080 (zone: us-west-1a)\n│  └─ Pod 10.1.2.1:9080 (zone: us-west-1b)\n├─ Cluster B (network: network-b)\n│  └─ Pod 10.2.1.1:9080 (zone: us-east-1a)\n```\n\nWhen a proxy in Cluster A requests endpoints:\n\n1. Local endpoints prioritized (same network)\n2. Remote endpoints included with gateway (cross-cluster LB traffic)\n3. Network labels used for traffic policies\n\n### Service Discovery Sources\n\n#### Kubernetes Registries\n\n- **K8s Services** → Service.Hostname, VIP\n- **K8s Endpoints** → IstioEndpoint list with pod labels/IPs\n- **K8s Pods** → Locality (zone labels), workload identity\n- **Labels** → Subset selection in DestinationRule\n\n#### ServiceEntry Resources\n\nCustom services (external, VMs):\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1alpha3\nkind: ServiceEntry\nmetadata:\n  name: external-api\nspec:\n  hosts:\n  - api.external.com\n  ports:\n  - number: 443\n    name: https\n  location: MESH_EXTERNAL\n  endpoints:\n  - address: 192.0.2.1  # External IP\n    ports:\n      https: 443\n```\n\nTreated like any other service, but endpoints are static/DNS.\n\n#### Workload Entry Resources\n\nVMs or external hosts:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1alpha3\nkind: WorkloadEntry\nmetadata:\n  name: vm-1\n  namespace: default\nspec:\n  address: 192.0.2.100\n  labels:\n    app: database\n    version: v1\n```\n\nMerged into service endpoints, participate in Sidecar and DestinationRule scoping.\n\n### Service Registry Triggers\n\nWhen endpoints change, `EDSUpdate` enqueues only affected proxies:\n\n```go\nfunc (s *DiscoveryServer) EDSUpdate(shard, hostname, namespace, endpoints)\n```\n\nThis avoids full pushes—only EDS (endpoint list) is recomputed:\n\n- **Trigger:** Pod scaled up/down, health changed\n- **Reason:** EndpointUpdate\n- **Full:** false (incremental update)\n- **ConfigsUpdated:** empty (means all proxies potentially affected, but only EDS recomputed)\n\nThis keeps endpoint changes fast and lightweight.\n\n---\n\n## Error Handling\n\n### Connection Errors\n\n**Scenarios:**\n- Proxy disconnects unexpectedly → `closeConnection()` removes from `adsClients`\n- Invalid metadata → return error, reject connection\n- Authorization fails → return error, deny access\n\n**Recovery:** Proxy reconnects with backoff; no state loss (stateless design)\n\n### Push Errors\n\n**Scenarios:**\n- Generator panics → recover and log error\n- Cache miss (shouldn't happen) → regenerate on the fly\n- Network error sending response → connection drops, proxy reconnects\n\n**Tracking:** Errors recorded in `PushContext.ProxyStatus[metricName]` for debugging\n\n### Config Errors\n\n**Scenarios:**\n- Invalid VirtualService → Validation errors logged but push continues\n- Circular service references → Detection and logging\n- Missing DestinationRule → Graceful fallback to default behavior\n\n**Mechanism:** Dedicated validation phase before push; errors recorded and exposed on debug endpoint\n\n---\n\n## Extension Points\n\n### Custom Generators\n\nOverride default resource generation:\n\n```go\nserver.Generators[\"my-custom\"] = &MyCustomGenerator{...}\n```\n\nProxy metadata `Generator: \"my-custom\"` triggers custom generator.\n\n### Custom Controllers\n\nAdd new service discovery sources:\n\n```go\nenv.ServiceDiscovery.(*aggregate.Controller).AddRegistry(myRegistry)\n```\n\nImplement `Controller` interface, register handlers.\n\n### ProxyNeedsPush Filter\n\nSkip pushes for proxies that don't need updates:\n\n```go\nserver.ProxyNeedsPush = func(proxy *model.Proxy, req *model.PushRequest) (*model.PushRequest, bool) {\n    // Custom logic: can we skip this push?\n    if req.Full && !proxy.NeedsFull() {\n        return req, false  // Skip\n    }\n    return req, true  // Send\n}\n```\n\n### EnvoyFilter Integration\n\nPatch generated Envoy config per proxy:\n\n```yaml\napiVersion: networking.istio.io/v1alpha3\nkind: EnvoyFilter\nmetadata:\n  name: auth-patch\nspec:\n  workloadSelector:\n    labels:\n      app: frontend\n  configPatches:\n  - applyTo: LISTENER\n    patch:\n      operation: MERGE\n      value:\n        # Merge custom listener config\n```\n\n---\n\n## Data Flow Example: Service Creation to Proxy Update\n\n### Scenario\nA new Kubernetes Service `shipping.default` is created with 2 pods.\n\n### Flow\n\n```\n1. Kubernetes API Server\n   └─ Event: Service \"shipping\" created\n\n2. K8s Service Registry Watcher\n   └─ Handler: onServiceCreate()\n\n3. XDSUpdater.SvcUpdate()\n   ├─ Reason: ServiceUpdate\n   └─ → DiscoveryServer.ConfigUpdate()\n\n4. DiscoveryServer.ConfigUpdate()\n   ├─ Increment InboundUpdates counter\n   ├─ Send to pushChannel\n   └─ (May be debounced)\n\n5. handleUpdates() (debounce goroutine)\n   ├─ Wait: DebounceAfter time since last event\n   ├─ Call: Push(req)\n   └─ Increment CommittedUpdates counter\n\n6. DiscoveryServer.Push()\n   ├─ Is Full push? YES (service is global config)\n   ├─ Call: initPushContext()\n   │  ├─ NewPushContext created\n   │  ├─ InitContext() called:\n   │  │  ├─ createNewContext() (or updateContext if incremental)\n   │  │  ├─ initServiceRegistry() loads Services (including new \"shipping\")\n   │  │  ├─ initDestinationRules()\n   │  │  ├─ initVirtualServices()\n   │  │  └─ initSidecarScopes() computes per-proxy visibility\n   │  └─ Store in Env.pushContext\n   ├─ dropCacheForRequest() clear old xDS cache\n   └─ Call: AdsPushAll(req)\n\n7. AdsPushAll()\n   ├─ For each proxy in adsClients:\n   │  └─ pushQueue.Enqueue(proxy, req)\n   └─ Proxies queued\n\n8. sendPushes() (push worker goroutine)\n   ├─ Acquire concurrentPushLimit semaphore\n   ├─ Dequeue proxy from pushQueue\n   ├─ Call: pushConnection(proxy, pushReq)\n   │  ├─ For each watched resource type (CDS, RDS, EDS):\n   │  │  ├─ Call: CdsGenerator.Generate(proxy, req)\n   │  │  │  ├─ Access: ps.ServiceIndex (includes new \"shipping\")\n   │  │  │  ├─ Filter by proxy SidecarScope (can proxy reach \"shipping\"?)\n   │  │  │  ├─ Transform: Service → Envoy Cluster\n   │  │  │  ├─ Cache result\n   │  │  │  └─ Return Cluster resources\n   │  │  └─ Build response\n   │  └─ Send DiscoveryResponse via gRPC\n   ├─ Queue marks done\n   └─ Release semaphore\n\n9. Envoy Proxy Receives\n   ├─ Response: clusters=[..., {name: \"outbound|80||shipping.default.svc.cluster.local\", ...}]\n   ├─ Version: \"2024-02-21T14:30:45Z/12345\"\n   ├─ Nonce: \"uuid-xyz\"\n   └─ Proxy ACKs with same nonce\n\n10. Proxy Configuration Updated\n    ├─ New cluster routing to \"shipping.default\" endpoints\n    ├─ Future requests sent to shipping pods\n    └─ No manual intervention needed\n```\n\n---\n\n## Key Performance Characteristics\n\n### Scalability Features\n\n1. **Incremental Pushes:** Only affected proxies enqueued (via SidecarScope filtering)\n2. **Debouncing:** Multiple config changes merged into single push\n3. **Concurrent Push Limiting:** Prevents thundering herd, steady resource usage\n4. **Request Rate Limiting:** Per-connection rate limiter on new xDS requests\n5. **Caching:** xDS resources cached by (proxy, resource-type, version)\n6. **Index Structures:** ServiceIndex, virtualServiceIndex, etc. enable O(1) lookups\n\n### Timing\n\n- **Debounce latency:** 100ms–10s (configurable)\n- **Push time:** Typically < 1s for 100-1000 proxies\n- **Connection init:** ~100ms (includes service target computation)\n\n---\n\n## Relevant Source Files\n\n### Core Components\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/discovery.go` — DiscoveryServer, push coordination, debouncing\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/ads.go` — ADS stream handler, Connection, request processing\n- `pilot/pkg/model/push_context.go` — PushContext, config initialization, indexes\n- `pilot/pkg/model/context.go` — Environment (push context holder)\n\n### Generators & Translation\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/cds.go` — Cluster Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/lds.go` — Listener Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/rds.go` — Route Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/eds.go` — Endpoint Discovery Service generator\n- `pilot/pkg/networking/core/` — ConfigGenerator (main translation logic)\n\n### Service Registry & Discovery\n- `pilot/pkg/model/service.go` — Service, ServiceInstance, Resolution types\n- `pilot/pkg/model/controller.go` — Controller interface, event model\n- `pilot/pkg/serviceregistry/` — Kubernetes controller implementation\n\n### Queue & Concurrency\n- `pilot/pkg/xds/pushqueue.go` — PushQueue, proxy push ordering\n\n### Configuration Models\n- `pilot/pkg/model/destination_rule.go` — DestinationRule handling\n- `pilot/pkg/model/virtualservice.go` — VirtualService handling\n- `pilot/pkg/model/sidecar.go` — SidecarScope computation\n- `pilot/pkg/model/authentication.go` — AuthN/AuthZ policy models\n\n### Multi-Cluster\n- `pilot/pkg/model/network.go` — Network topology for multi-cluster\n- Cluster registry aggregation in ServiceDiscovery\n\n---\n\n## Conclusion\n\nPilot's Discovery Service achieves high performance and scalability through:\n\n1. **Separation of concerns:** DiscoveryServer handles gRPC; PushContext holds config snapshot; Generators handle translation\n2. **Smart filtering:** SidecarScope limits what each proxy needs; incremental pushes reduce work\n3. **Debouncing & rate limiting:** Prevents thrashing when many config changes occur\n4. **Pluggable architecture:** Custom generators, controllers, and filters enable extensions\n5. **Stateless design:** Proxies reconnect without data loss; connection state rebuilt from config\n\nThe architecture elegantly scales to thousands of proxies and supports advanced features like multi-cluster, ambient mesh, and traffic management with minimal manual tuning.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
